23:39:53 run T8f 20260921-233953: Why did one first-light release wait ten days for DF's wave? Three of four dawn replicates (T8d, T8e) got DF's wave inside the week of the day-1 release; the fourth waited ten days while five gated badgers from spring still stood. Hypothesis: a standing gated land group from the prior season delays DF's next draw after a release. Two arms on T8d's dawn design: HELD keeps the oldest gated land group on the map across the boundary (held 60 days at Spring day 70+); CLEAR dismisses every land group at Spring day 70+ so the boundary is met with no gated group. Measured: ticks from summer day 1's 'the gate opens' to the first surface arrival.
